A grandfather, on the other hand, is a wise, experienced, and respected individual who has lived through times that were vastly different from those we live in today. A grandfather is someone who has seen wars, economic depressions, and social changes that have shaped the world we know today. A grandfather is someone who has gained a wealth of knowledge and insights that are invaluable to learn from, share, and store. A grandfather is someone who will offer time-tested advice, guidance, and support that will be invaluable in framing your understanding of the world and your place in it. An iPad kid, on the other hand, will only provide short, fleeting moments of entertainment and accompaniment, but will not offer any lasting value or wisdom
